# Awesome List for Claude Connectors with stars

<p align="center">
  <img src="media/banner.svg" alt="Awesome Claude Connectors" width="800">
</p>

> A comprehensive directory of the connectors in Anthropic's [Claude Connectors catalog](https://www.anthropic.com/partners/mcp) — 2,562 MCP integrations across both catalog surfaces (the curated web directory and the in-app catalog, which additionally surfaces community-built and desktop-extension connectors), plus 39 held pending vendor verification, organized by category with descriptions and use cases.

**Last updated:** September 14, 2026 | **Connectors tracked:** 2,562 listed + 39 held | **Categories:** 30

Claude connectors are MCP (Model Context Protocol) servers that extend Claude with real-time access to external tools, data sources, and services. They work across Claude.ai, Claude Desktop, Claude Mobile, Claude Code, and Claude Cowork. Connectors in the **curated web directory** are vetted by Anthropic for security, reliability, and compatibility; the **in-app catalog** additionally surfaces community-built and local desktop-extension connectors that Anthropic makes available but does not itself build or vet. This list tracks the union of both surfaces — see CONTRIBUTING.md for the two-surface methodology.

For more information, see the [Connectors Documentation](https://claude.com/docs/connectors/directory), [Submission Guidelines](https://claude.com/docs/connectors/building/submission), and [MCP Protocol Specification](https://modelcontextprotocol.io).

Connectors marked with **`A`** are built and maintained by Anthropic. Connectors marked with **`C`** carry the in-app catalog's Community badge — published in the catalog by Anthropic but built by third parties and not vetted the way web-directory entries are. `C` markers currently reflect the 2026-09-14 catalog capture, taken from the directory feed's verified tier (community → `C`, partner → none) and re-synced every sweep.

This list is maintained weekly. To contribute, see [CONTRIBUTING.md](CONTRIBUTING.md).

> This is an independent, community-maintained list. Not affiliated with, endorsed by, or sponsored by Anthropic PBC. "Claude" and related marks are the property of Anthropic PBC. Each connector is the property of its respective owner.

> \[!TIP]
>
> ### Connector Snap Stack — September 14, 2026
>
> Each sweep features a persona and a small stack of connectors that click together. Past stacks are archived in [docs/stacks](docs/stacks/); sweep-by-sweep history lives in the [changelog](docs/CHANGELOG.md).
>
> **The one-person supply chain** — Terminal49 *(new)* · Stord *(new)* · LetMeShip Next *(new)* · PostCo *(new)* · running in **Claude Desktop**
>
> Tuesday, one Desktop conversation: ask Terminal49 which inbound containers are still sitting at the port and pull the event history behind the one that slipped, check Stord for stock at the facility about to run the promotion and which retailer missed on-time-in-full last week, price the pallet to Spain in LetMeShip with duties estimated before committing, then rank PostCo's return reasons by SKU and ask the question none of the four dashboards can answer alone — is the SKU we're about to reorder the one customers keep sending back. Four connectors that each see one leg of the chain, with Claude holding the whole route. Composed, not yet field-tested — if you run it against a live operation, send a Field Report (see CONTRIBUTING).

## AI and ML

71 connectors.

* [Agensi](https://www.agensi.io) **`C`** - Search, discover, and install AI agent skills. *Use case: Searching for a skill that drafts release notes, installing an agent skill for meeting summaries, checking what's new in the catalog this week.*
* [Agently](https://www.agently.dev) **`C`** - Give your agents your company's memory. *Use case: Asking an agent what pricing was quoted to a customer last month, pulling up a decision made in a past project, checking a policy the team agreed to earlier this year.*
* [Ai for Work](https://www.kore.ai/ai-for-work) **`C`** - Search across silos, automate workflows, and orchestrate AI agents. *Use case: Finding a contract buried across shared drives and email, triggering a workflow when a deal closes, coordinating agents on a quarterly report.*
* [AI Passport](https://ego.ist) **`C`** - Keeps a portable memory layer across AI apps where saves arrive as proposals for approval and each app gets time-limited, category-scoped access. *Use case: Approving which facts from a session become durable memory, granting one assistant a week of work context, reviewing the activity receipts afterwards.*
* [app.cotera.co](https://cotera.co) **`C`** - Builds Cotera agents from markdown prompts, searches connected tools and documents, and runs agents against a single input or up to 50 inputs in parallel. *Use case: Drafting an agent that summarizes support tickets, running it over a batch of transcripts, reading a teammate's existing agent prompt before reusing it.*
* [Arkon Vault](https://arkoncybersecurity.com) **`C`** - Keep every AI on the same project, vision, and next step. *Use case: Catching an agent that drifted off the agreed plan, confirming the next step matches the stated vision, checking what a previous session decided before starting a new one.*
* [Arroway](https://www.arroway.app) **`C`** - Stores a team's shared rules, decisions, facts, and references for AI assistants to read, with changes to approved content proposed for human review. *Use case: Checking a team's standing conventions before proposing a plan, appending a record of what a session changed, proposing an edit to an approved rule.*
* [AskNeedl](https://needl.ai) **`C`** - Enterprise AI assistant that searches internal and external data sources and returns cited answers. *Use case: Ask a question across company documents, filings, and news feeds to get a sourced answer.*
* [Brizz](https://www.brizz.ai) **`C`** - Product analytics for AI agents that captures agent conversations, groups them into issues, and surfaces silent failures and user requests. *Use case: Reviewing which questions an agent failed to answer last week, grouping repeat complaints into one issue, spotting the request users keep making.*
* [Cloudglue](https://cloudglue.dev) - Video analysis and processing. *Use case: Extracting insights from video content, automated video summarization.*
* [Coconut Context](https://coconut.dev) **`C`** - Gives humans and agents one versioned source of truth in markdown, readable and writable across every Claude surface via MCP. *Use case: Pulling up a decision logged last week from a different device, editing a shared markdown doc mid-conversation, handing an agent the same context a teammate already set up.*
* [Context Switcher](https://reasoning.services/tools/context-switcher) **`C`** - Helps Claude reframe complex problems by swapping between predefined perspectives, criteria sets, and decision contexts on demand. *Use case: Running a pricing decision through a risk-averse lens then a growth lens, switching criteria sets mid-conversation to see how the recommendation changes.*
* [Contextaco](https://contextaco.com) **`C`** - Captures a session's decisions, constraints, failed approaches, and open questions as addressable note sets that later sessions load, fork, or publish. *Use case: Saving the reasoning behind an architecture choice at the end of a session, loading it before the next one, forking it when the work branches.*
* [Contextick](https://contextick.ai) **`C`** - Saves text such as decisions, ideas, and snippets to a private store and recalls the closest match by meaning across connected AI assistants. *Use case: Saving a team naming convention once, recalling a past architecture decision mid-task, pulling back a snippet stored from a different assistant.*
* [Coval](https://www.coval.ai) **`C`** - Test and improve AI agents with Coval. *Use case: Running a batch of test conversations against an agent before shipping it, checking where a test run failed, comparing two versions of an agent's responses to the same prompt.*
* [ElevenLabs](https://elevenlabs.io) - Voice AI platform for generating speech and building conversational voice agents. *Use case: Create and manage ElevenLabs voice agents without leaving the chat interface.*
* [ElevenLabs Agents MCP App](https://elevenlabs.io/conversational-ai) - Build conversational AI agents with voice. *Use case: Voice-enabled AI assistants, phone agents, voice-first applications.*
* [ElevenLabs Player](https://elevenlabs.io/text-to-speech) - Audio generation and playback. *Use case: Text-to-speech, audio content creation, voiceover generation.*
* [elluminate](https://www.ellamind.com/products/elluminate) **`C`** - AI Evaluation & Observation Platform by ellamind. *Use case: Checking why a model's output quality dropped this week, comparing evaluation scores across two versions, reviewing a log of flagged responses.*
* [Expertise AI](https://www.expertise.ai) **`C`** - Uploads skills built in Claude to an Expertise account, previewing each import, skipping unchanged content on re-import, and listing what has been imported. *Use case: Pushing a newly built skill to a team account, previewing what an import would create before running it, re-syncing after an edit.*
* [FidelicAI: Hire AI Agents](https://fidelic.ai) **`C`** - Matches business work to published FidelicAI agent roles, shows each role's public rates and limits, and opens the hiring path. *Use case: Describing a backlog of work to see which role fits, comparing a day pass against hiring a person, starting the hire once a role is chosen.*
* [Flowera](https://flowera.ai) **`C`** - Builds, edits, and test-runs WhatsApp, Instagram, and web chat assistants in a Flowera workspace, and reviews conversations, captured leads, and webhooks. *Use case: Describing a support assistant and having its flow wired up, sending a test message to see what it answers, reading leads captured overnight.*
* [Graffiticode](https://graffiticode.org) **`C`** - An open toolbox of task-specific AI tools. *Use case: Calling a purpose-built tool for a narrow task, chaining task-specific tools into an agent workflow, extending an agent with a new tool.*
* [Great Arrow Digital](https://www.greatarrow.ai) **`C`** - One shared memory for every AI you use. *Use case: Asking a different AI assistant about a preference set earlier this week, checking what one tool already knows before repeating it to another.*
* [Hugging Face](https://huggingface.co) - Access Hugging Face Hub models and Gradio apps. *Use case: Model exploration, running inference on hosted models, discovering pretrained models for ML workflows.*
* [Jockey by TwelveLabs](https://www.twelvelabs.io/jockey) **`C`** - Understand, search, and connect your entire video and photo library. *Use case: Finding every clip where a specific product appears on screen, searching a photo library by scene description, connecting related videos from different shoots.*
* [Kavara Kirk](https://kavara.ai) **`C`** - Structural change detection on raw, unlabeled data, CPU-only, with no training required. *Use case: Flagging where a sensor log shifted behavior without any labeled examples, running a change-detection pass on a laptop CPU, checking raw data for a structural break before deciding whether to retrain anything.*
* [Kemory by SeKondBrain](https://www.sekondbrain.ai) **`C`** - Stores preferences, project facts, and decisions as permissioned memory shared across MCP clients, with per-user scoping, deletion, and audit logging. *Use case: Recalling a decision made last week in a different tool, promoting a project fact so teammates can use it, deleting a preference that no longer applies.*
* [Keynodex Memory](https://keynodex.com/memory) **`C`** - Personal durable memory, portable across AIs. *Use case: Bringing a preference noted in one AI tool into a conversation with another, checking a fact saved weeks earlier without re-explaining it.*
* [Kinetik](https://kineto.app) **`C`** - Lists the Kinetik agents a user can reach, delegates work to them, and returns their replies, enforcing each agent's owner-set access level. *Use case: Handing a weekly content plan to a trained agent, asking a shared agent about the knowledge its owner gave it, refining a draft over follow-up turns.*
* [Komplyt Zero](https://www.kompl.yt/zero) **`C`** - Stop briefing and start working. *Use case: Handing off a task without writing a lengthy brief first, letting an agent start on the work immediately.*
* [LeapMemory](https://leapmemory.com) **`C`** - Permanent, private memory for Claude. *Use case: Picking up a conversation from weeks ago without repeating the backstory, keeping a personal note that only one account can see.*
* [Lenz](https://lenz.io) **`C`** - An independent fact-checking layer for AI output that returns a sourced verdict for any factual claim, either a fast check or the full eight-model trace. *Use case: Checking whether a claim in a draft article is actually true, verifying a statistic before it goes into a report, running the full eight-model trace on a contested fact instead of a quick pass.*
* [MCP Memory](https://mnemoverse.com/docs/api/mcp-server) - Hosted persistent memory for AI agents with one key across Claude Code, Cursor, VS Code, and ChatGPT. *Use case: Carrying a decision made in a Claude Code session into a Cursor session the next day, recalling a preference stored weeks earlier before drafting a reply, marking a remembered fact as wrong so it fades.*
* [Meko](https://mekodata.ai) - Shared context for agents that work and learn together. *Use case: Letting one agent pick up context another agent already gathered, keeping multiple agents working from the same shared notes as a project evolves.*
* [Mem0](https://mem0.ai) - Persistent memory for AI agents and assistants. *Use case: Long-term memory that survives across sessions, semantic recall of prior context, shared memory across tools.*
* [Memories.ai](https://memories.ai) **`C`** - Search, recall & analyze your video memories. *Use case: Finding the clip where a specific person appears across old footage, recalling what happened during a trip last summer, analyzing a video for a recurring pattern.*
* [MemoryPlugin](https://www.memoryplugin.com) **`C`** - One shared, permanent memory for every AI you use. *Use case: Asking a different AI tool about a fact saved months ago, keeping a preference consistent no matter which assistant is used that day.*
* [Mixer4AI](https://locusconsulting.com) **`C`** - Publishes machine-readable capability manifests for attested business AI agents and browses or posts structured intents such as needs, RFPs, and problem statements. *Use case: Listing an agent's capabilities for others to find, posting an RFP into the exchange, searching published intents that match what an agent offers.*
* [Ollang](https://ollang.com) **`C`** - The AI Language Execution Platform -- Enterprise Localization. *Use case: Localizing a product's interface text for a multi-language enterprise rollout, running a translation batch through the platform before a release.*
* [Omni by xpander](https://xpander.ai) **`C`** - Builds, runs, and monitors xpander.ai agents that reach internal systems and private APIs, execute long-running tasks on the platform, and leave an audit trail. *Use case: Describing a reconciliation job to wire into an agent, delegating work to an agent another team published, checking what a long-running task cost.*
* [Open Context](https://opnctx.ai) **`C`** - Stores profiles, documents, and instructions as reusable context and loads the relevant pieces into a conversation on request. *Use case: Saving a client profile once and reusing it across sessions, loading a style guide before drafting, pulling standing instructions into a new conversation.*
* [Orbismo](https://orbismo.com) **`C`** - Build collaborative worlds with AI. *Use case: Maintaining a novel's character and timeline canon, tracking a game campaign's world lore, keeping a knowledge base of people and relationships current.*
* [Owkin](https://owkin.com) - AI agents for biology. *Use case: Biological research, computational biology workflows, drug discovery.*
* [Parallel Search](https://parallel.ai) - Free token-efficient web search built for AI agents. *Use case: Grounding answers with a search API tuned for LLM consumption, low-token retrieval in agent pipelines.*
* [Perseus Vault — Persistent Memory for Claude](https://perseus.observer/vault) - Perseus Vault is a local-first, single-binary memory server that gives AI agents persistent, encrypted, bi-temporal memory across sessions via more than 50 MCP tools. *Use case: Store a project decision as an entity so a future session can recall it instead of re-deriving it.*
* [POMA Grill](https://www.poma-ai.com/products/grill) **`C`** - Turn any file into AI-searchable context. *Use case: Making a scanned PDF searchable within a conversation, turning a folder of spreadsheets into context an agent can query, feeding a slide deck in so it can be asked about later.*
* [Primate Vision](https://primateintelligence.ai) **`C`** - Answers questions about video files and URLs, returning detected objects, actions, and events with frame locations and timestamps. *Use case: Finding when a forklift entered an aisle in warehouse footage, counting workers wearing hard hats in a safety clip, logging what happens after a door opens.*
* [production.pretty-prompt.com](https://pretty-prompt.com) **`C`** - Stores, refines and iterates on prompts in one Pretty Prompt workspace. *Use case: Saving a prompt that finally worked, rewriting an older version after a model change, comparing two variants before picking one.*
* [Qontext](https://qontext.ai) **`C`** - Self-updating company context repository that AI agents can search, read, and edit over MCP. *Use case: Pull the latest account facts on a customer or deal directly into a Claude conversation instead of gathering context by hand.*
* [Rapidata](https://www.rapidata.ai) **`C`** - Collect real human feedback from Rapidata's global crowd without leaving Claude. *Use case: Running a quick preference poll on two logo options, getting real people to rate a product description before publishing, collecting reactions to a survey question overnight.*
* [Roboflow](https://roboflow.com) - Build, search, and run computer vision models. *Use case: Searching a model catalog, running inference on an image, managing a training dataset.*
* [Seltz](https://seltz.ai) **`C`** - Real-time web search for AI agents, from an index built for AI agents. *Use case: Checking today's breaking news before drafting a summary, pulling the latest pricing page for a competitor, verifying a fact that changed since the last training cutoff.*
* [Sidecloud.ai Platform](https://sidecloud.ai) **`C`** - Turns document collections into structured, traceable knowledge that agents can search and navigate through Sidecloud's managed Knowledge Base. *Use case: Searching an indexed document set for a policy detail, tracing an answer back to its source document, navigating a knowledge base by structure.*
* [skilder](https://www.skilder.ai) **`C`** - One place to build, share, and govern the skills and tools your AI agents use at work. *Use case: Publishing a new skill for the sales team to reuse, reviewing which tools an agent is allowed to call before a rollout, retiring an outdated skill that's no longer approved.*
* [SkilzVolt](https://skilzvolt.com) **`C`** - Governed catalogue of approved company skills and procedures that connected agents search and read, with change proposals, review modes, immutable versions, and audit trails. *Use case: Looking up the approved procedure before running a task, proposing a revision after finding a gap, checking which version a workspace signed off on.*
* [Smara](https://prajana.ai) **`C`** - Gives agents durable memory: consolidated facts, a knowledge graph, episodic events, and a scratchpad, retrieved by hybrid vector, graph, and recency ranking. *Use case: Recalling what an agent learned about a customer last month, recording a decision mid-task for later turns, reviewing what happened in an earlier session.*
* [SoupNet](https://www.soup.net) **`C`** - Records stated preferences and past judgment calls in shareable recipe books and returns related prior decisions when an agent checks a hypothesis. *Use case: Checking a stored preference before an agent picks a tone, recording a decision so other tools follow it, sharing a recipe book with a collaborator.*
* [Speko](https://speko.ai) - Reads a Speko voice-AI account — agents, calls, transcripts, recordings, phone numbers, credits, and usage — and transcribes supplied audio. *Use case: Pulling the transcript of a call that went wrong, checking credit balance before a campaign, turning an uploaded recording into text.*
* [Stem](https://awase.dev) **`C`** - Stores distilled memories — decisions, facts, preferences, project context — recalls relevant ones in later chats, and links them into a typed knowledge graph. *Use case: Saving a stack decision so the next session starts informed, recalling a client's stated preferences months later, linking a new note to the project it belongs to.*
* [Synthflow](https://synthflow.ai) - Build and manage AI voice agents from chat. *Use case: Setting up a voice agent to handle inbound support calls, updating a voice agent's script after a product change, checking why a call transferred to the wrong flow.*
* [Talarion](https://talarion.com) **`C`** - Keep your AI up to date. *Use case: Refreshing an assistant's knowledge after a product changes, checking whether an AI's information is stale before relying on it in a meeting.*
* [Tempreon](https://tempreon.com) **`C`** - Carries your identity, preferences, and decision rules into every AI you use, and learns from every session so your AI compounds. *Use case: Applying a decision rule from one AI tool to a new assistant without restating it, carrying a stated preference into a fresh session, watching a recommendation improve after repeated use.*
* [ToolUniverse](https://tooluniverse.org) - Access 600+ scientific tools. *Use case: Scientific computing, running domain-specific tools for chemistry, physics, and biology research.*
* [TruthScan](https://truthscan.com) **`C`** - Determines whether an image is AI-generated, photoshopped, or authentic using TruthScan's detection model. *Use case: Checking a submitted profile photo for AI generation, screening a press image before republishing, verifying a product photo flagged as suspicious.*
* [Unabyss](https://unabyss.com) **`C`** - Shared memory for every AI tool you use. *Use case: Checking what a different AI tool already knows before repeating a preference, keeping an ongoing project's details consistent across separate assistants.*
* [Valossa Assistant](https://valossa.com/video-ai-assistant) **`C`** - Ask your videos anything — get answers, soundbites and clips, inside Claude. *Use case: Asking which scene in a raw interview mentions a competitor by name, pulling a 10-second soundbite for a highlight reel, getting a clip of the moment a guest laughs.*
* [Vertiso Memory](https://memory.vertiso.ai) - Portable, durable memory for every AI agent you use. *Use case: Bringing a fact saved in one agent into a conversation with a different one, checking a note from last month without re-entering it.*
* [Weave Fabric](https://weave.cloud) **`C`** - Your life's context, in conversation. *Use case: Mentioning a family trip planned for next month without re-explaining who's going, referencing a personal detail from an earlier conversation without repeating it.*
* [Wisdom Recall](https://wisdomrecall.com) **`C`** - Provides guided prompt templates for work, money, health, family, and learning, and saves user-owned memory notes for reuse in other AI tools. *Use case: Picking a guided prompt before a difficult money decision, saving a note about a recurring health question, carrying saved context into another assistant.*
* [Wolfram](https://www.wolframalpha.com) - Inject precise computation and curated knowledge. *Use case: Symbolic math, unit conversions, real-world data lookups, scientific calculation, and step-by-step problem solving against Wolfram's knowledge engine.*

## Automation and Integration

39 connectors.

* [Ace Work](https://aceworkflow.io/ace-work) **`C`** - Capture workflows by talking to Claude, run your SOPs step by step, and log where reality differs. *Use case: Walking through an onboarding process out loud so a workflow gets recorded automatically, running a shipping SOP step by step during an actual shift, flagging the point where the actual process skipped a documented step.*
* [app.hookie.ai](https://hookie.ai) **`C`** - Builds and inspects Hookie webhook pipelines: endpoints, routing rules, datasets, destinations, ingest keys, delivery history, and replays. *Use case: Finding why last night's delivery returned an error code, adding a routing rule for a new provider event, replaying a failed delivery.*
* [Azava](https://azava.com) **`C`** - Automations over your tools, built by asking Claude. *Use case: Asking for a workflow that moves a new signup from a form into a CRM automatically, building an automation that files a support ticket when a keyword appears, having an agent wire two tools together without writing the integration by hand.*
* [Ballet](https://ballet.dev) **`C`** - Builds and runs Ballet AI agents and multi-step playbooks, connects them to other systems over MCP, and triggers workflows from schedules, webhooks, and forms. *Use case: Chaining a research agent into a lead-enrichment playbook, scheduling a weekly report run, wiring a form submission to start an intake workflow.*
* [CData Connect AI](https://www.cdata.com) - Managed MCP for 350+ data sources. *Use case: Universal data connector, querying databases and SaaS APIs through a single interface.*
* [Celigo](https://www.celigo.com) - Build and manage automations across hundreds of apps. *Use case: Connecting a new order in an e-commerce app to a shipping tool automatically, fixing a broken automation between two apps before it drops data, adding a new app to an existing workflow without rebuilding it.*
* [cloud.activepieces.com](https://www.activepieces.com) **`C`** - Open-source automation platform whose flows, triggers, steps, branches, tables, and integrations can be listed, created, published, and test-run through conversation. *Use case: Adding a conditional branch to an existing flow, publishing a draft automation after a test run, checking which integrations a workspace already supports.*
* [CodeWords](https://codewords.ai) - Build AI agents and automations by chatting with AI. *Use case: Building a custom automation workflow, creating an agent without code, chaining tools together via chat.*
* [DecisionRules](https://www.decisionrules.io) **`C`** - Creates, versions, and tests decision tables, lookup tables, and scripting rules in DecisionRules, and evaluates published or draft rules against input data. *Use case: Generating a pricing decision table from a written policy, running a test suite before publishing a rule, checking what a rule returns for a sample record.*
* [Envia Shipping](https://envia.com) **`C`** - Quotes carrier rates, buys and voids labels, tracks packages, schedules pickups, and generates customs paperwork across more than 250 carriers. *Use case: Comparing rates for an international parcel before buying a label, scheduling a carrier pickup for the afternoon, classifying HS codes for a customs invoice.*
* [FreightPOP](https://www.freightpop.com) **`C`** - Quotes freight rates across carriers, books and cancels shipments, tracks status, imports orders, validates addresses, and checks warehouse inventory in FreightPOP. *Use case: Comparing carrier quotes for a two-pallet load, booking the winning rate, confirming stock on hand before promising a ship date.*
* [Hiveflow](https://hiveflow.ai) **`C`** - Lists, inspects, creates, and triggers Hiveflow AI workflows, manages kanban boards and cards, and reports the status of agents watching those boards. *Use case: Running a scheduled flow on demand after a data fix, moving a card into a watched column to hand work to an agent, checking which workers last reported in.*
* [IFTTT](https://ifttt.com) - Automate 1000+ apps. *Use case: Cross-app automation, trigger-based workflows connecting disparate services.*
* [Inistate](https://inistate.com) **`C`** - Creates business workflows and runs them through structured forms: updating records, triggering approvals, uploading files, querying entries, and executing state transitions, with audit trails. *Use case: Moving a request to the next approval stage, attaching a signed document to a record, pulling the entries still waiting on review.*
* [Jentic](https://jentic.com) - Universal tool access. *Use case: Connecting to APIs without writing integration code.*

66 connectors.

* [mcp.hologrow.ai](https://hologrow.ai) **`C`** - Provides read-only analysis of connected commerce, advertising, analytics, and ERP data from sources including Amazon Seller Central, Google Ads, GA4, and Meta Ads. *Use case: Comparing ad spend against marketplace revenue by channel, investigating a sudden drop in paid conversions, assembling a monthly report from several sources.*
* [MerchantSpring](https://merchantspring.io) **`C`** - Marketplace intelligence for agencies and brands - sales, profit, advertising, inventory, traffic, Buy Box, and customer analytics across Amazon, Walmart, eBay, and 120+ channels. *Use case: Checking which channel is most profitable this month, seeing if a listing lost the Buy Box on Amazon, comparing inventory levels across Walmart and eBay before restocking.*
* [Metorik](https://metorik.com) **`C`** - Real-time analytics for your Shopify or WooCommerce store - sales, profit, costs, customers, and forecasts. *Use case: Checking today's profit margin after a big sale, forecasting next month's revenue, seeing which customers are repeat buyers.*
* [New Vintage](https://www.newvintage.ai) **`C`** - Brings a winery's Commerce7, RedChirp, Klaviyo, and Mailchimp data together for reporting, customer segmentation, and identifying revenue opportunities. *Use case: Reporting quarterly sales by channel, segmenting customers who stopped ordering, checking which campaign list produced the most revenue.*
* [Pattern Intelligence](https://www.pattern.com) **`C`** - Answers questions about ecommerce brand performance on Amazon, Walmart, and other marketplaces, covering buy box health, price suppression, inventory gaps, and market trends. *Use case: Checking which listings lost the buy box this week, spotting titles at risk of price suppression, reviewing where inventory gaps are costing marketplace sales.*
* [PostCo](https://postco.co) **`C`** - Gives read-only access to a retailer's PostCo Return Centre data, covering return volumes, reasons, refunds, exchanges, store credit, and shipments. *Use case: Ranking last quarter's top return reasons by SKU, checking how much store credit was issued in March, tracing an exchange that never shipped.*
* [Pricewatcha](https://pricewatcha.com) **`C`** - Turn any product URL into price intelligence. *Use case: Pasting in a product link to see how its price has moved over time, checking if a listed price is actually a good deal.*
* [Product Research](https://oneproduct.dev) **`C`** - Returns Amazon market data for a product idea, keyword, ASIN, or seller, covering search volume, pricing, competition, Buy Box contention, and seller details. *Use case: Sizing demand for a new kitchen gadget, checking how crowded a category already is, pulling the keywords a competing listing ranks for.*
* [Sellergoo](https://sellergoo.com) **`C`** - Reads computed e-commerce profit after fees, refunds, cost of goods, and ads: KPIs, monthly P\&L, per-order economics, margins, payouts, and stock. *Use case: Finding which SKUs lost money last month, checking a payout against the orders behind it, reviewing month-by-month profit after ad spend.*
* [Sellerise](https://sellerise.com) **`C`** - Analyze Amazon seller business data. *Use case: Tracking Amazon sales trends, monitoring profit margins, auditing inventory levels.*
* [ShopMCP](https://shop-mcp.app) **`C`** - Read-only access to a brand's connected ecommerce stack, spanning store, analytics, ads, support, and finance data for trading, product, retention, and inventory questions. *Use case: Diagnosing a sudden drop in weekly revenue, checking which products are drifting out of stock, tracing a retention dip back to a support theme.*
* [Triple Whale](https://triplewhale.com) **`C`** - Real-time e-commerce analytics across every channel. *Use case: Tracking e-commerce attribution, monitoring ad spend ROI, unifying multi-channel revenue data.*
* [TrueGradient](https://www.truegradient.ai) **`C`** - Read-only access to a TrueGradient workspace's supply-chain experiment datasets covering demand planning, inventory optimization, and pricing, with aggregation queries. *Use case: Ranking categories by potential sales loss, checking projected out-of-stock days for top items, reviewing where last quarter's forecasts ran biased.*
* [TrueProfit](https://trueprofit.io) **`C`** - Connect TrueProfit to Claude. *Use case: Connecting an account to pull its data into a conversation instead of switching apps.*
* [Vasco](https://vasco.app) **`C`** - Unifies revenue data from CRM, billing, and product systems into one graph with resolved accounts, user-authored metric definitions, plan targets, and variance. *Use case: Confirming a Stripe customer and a HubSpot company are one account, checking quarter-to-date revenue against plan, reviewing the definition behind a reported metric.*
* [Vortex IQ](https://www.vortexiq.ai) **`C`** - AI operating system for ecommerce that monitors store performance, KPIs, and alerts across platforms like Shopify and BigCommerce. *Use case: Ask for a summary of this week's store KPI anomalies and alerts before a Monday standup.*
* [Yokitup](https://www.yokitup.com) **`C`** - Queries restaurant products, recipes, food costs, inventories, waste, stock transfers, and sales, combining them for cross-location operational analysis. *Use case: Identifying which products generate the most waste across sites, comparing food cost against sales for a menu item, investigating an unexplained stock transfer.*
* [YourBI](https://yourbi.nl) **`C`** - Analyse your hospitality data with Claude. *Use case: Checking a hospitality property's occupancy for last weekend, comparing revenue across two properties this month.*

### Company and Market Intelligence

* [Anthropic Economic Index](https://www.anthropic.com/economic-index) **`A`** - A public research initiative from Anthropic that publishes data and analysis on how Claude is used across the economy. *Use case: Exploring aggregated usage patterns to see which occupations and tasks are most affected by AI adoption.*
* [Bassethound](https://bassethound.ai) **`C`** - Sniff out deep company intelligence on any domain. *Use case: Researching a company before a sales call, starting from nothing but its domain.*
* [Cala](https://www.cala.ai) **`C`** - Verified, typed facts connected in one external graph for agents that need reliable data. *Use case: Checking a fact against a verified external graph before an agent acts on it, pulling a typed record other agents can reuse.*
* [Canonical Company Search](https://trycanonical.ai) **`C`** - Searches a company corpus by plain-English ICP or seed domain and returns domain-keyed records with funding, founders, employee count, geography, and corporate-family links. *Use case: Building a shortlist of niche competitors from a description, resolving a company name to its domain, checking a target's last funding round before outreach.*
* [Carbon Arc](https://www.carbonarc.co) **`C`** - Data infrastructure for the AI economy. *Use case: Sourcing external datasets for AI models, integrating third-party data feeds, building data pipelines.*
* [CE Cosmos](https://www.consumeredge.com) **`C`** - Near-real-time consumer spend data from millions of US cards. Track brand performance, benchmark competitors, and spot inflections. Listed as separate Deep Dive and Signal connectors in the catalog. *Use case: Tracking brand performance, benchmarking competitors, and spotting inflections using near-real-time consumer card spend data.*
* [CKAN MCP Server](https://ckan.org) - Access open data portals. *Use case: Querying government and public datasets, open data exploration for research.*
* [Company Registry Lookup](https://registry-lookup.com) **`C`** - Search 521M+ legal entities across 310 jurisdictions. *Use case: Looking up a company's legal registration status before signing a contract, checking if a foreign entity is registered in its jurisdiction.*
* [Comscore](https://www.comscore.com) **`C`** - Query Comscore's cross-platform audience measurement - digital and linear. *Use case: Comparing digital and linear audience reach for a media plan, checking cross-platform measurement before a campaign pitch.*
* [Cookiedeal MCP](https://cookiedeal.io) **`C`** - Searches Korean company records, OpenDART disclosures, financial and ownership data, view-only corporate and real-estate registry PDFs, and a member's business-card network. *Use case: Checking a Korean supplier's latest disclosure filing, pulling ownership structure before a partnership call, looking up a contact from a saved business card.*
* [Crustdata](https://crustdata.com) - Search and enrich B2B company and people data. *Use case: Building a prospect list, enriching a CRM record, researching a company's headcount and funding.*
* [Dcipher Analytics](https://www.dcipheranalytics.com) **`C`** - Runs Dcipher research agents over web, news, social, and uploaded content, builds searchable knowledge bases, and produces trend, comparison, and scenario views. *Use case: Collecting coverage on a market shift, asking a knowledge base a question with references back to sources, building a trend radar for a quarterly review.*
* [ECDB](https://ecdb.com) **`C`** - Queries e-commerce market intelligence on retailer revenues, market sizes, category rankings, and conversion metrics across more than 100 countries. *Use case: Sizing a category before entering a new country, benchmarking a competitor's revenue growth, comparing conversion rates across two markets.*
* [exascale.build](https://exascale.build) **`C`** - Source-cited dataset of US power, infrastructure, and AI-economy statistics built for querying by agents. *Use case: Pull sourced figures on US data center power capacity for a market analysis.*
* [Firmenbuch](https://www.agentic-firmenbuch.at) **`C`** - Das österreichisches Firmenbuch für KI-Agenten – Stammdaten, Bilanzen & Kennzahlen. *Use case: Vor einer Kreditprüfung die Bilanz eines Lieferanten abrufen, die Stammdaten eines neuen Geschäftspartners prüfen, Kennzahlen zweier Firmen vergleichen.*
* [Global Database](https://www.globaldatabase.com) **`C`** - Turns a company name into full company insight and data. *Use case: Researching a prospective business partner, verifying a company's financial standing, building a target list for outreach.*
* [Iceflower](https://iceflower.ai) **`C`** - Real world market insights about your customers and competitors. *Use case: Checking what a competitor changed recently, understanding a shift in customer behavior before a strategy meeting.*
* [iSpv](https://ispv.ro) **`C`** - Romanian company and financial data. *Use case: Looking up a Romanian company's filings, checking a partner's registration status, researching a competitor's financials.*
* [Meltwater](https://www.meltwater.com) - Search and analyze billions of news articles and social posts. *Use case: Monitoring brand mentions across media, tracking social sentiment, researching competitor coverage.*
* [Newsflash](https://newsflash.sh) **`C`** - Real-time corroborated news events + multi-year archive. *Use case: Checking if a breaking news claim has been corroborated by multiple sources, searching the archive for how a story developed over the past few years.*

* [Pappers France](https://www.pappers.fr) **`C`** - Données entreprises, justice, immobilier & politique. *Use case: Vérifier la situation judiciaire d'une entreprise avant de signer un contrat, consulter les données immobilières d'un bien avant un achat, retrouver les mandats politiques d'un dirigeant.*
* [PredictLeads](https://predictleads.com) **`C`** - Get real-time structured company signals: job postings, news, tech stacks, financing events. *Use case: Checking if a target account just posted a new engineering role, seeing what tech stack a prospect is running, spotting a company's latest funding round before a sales call.*

* [Soundcharts](https://soundcharts.com) **`C`** - Actionable Data for the Music Industry. *Use case: Pulling a data point about the music industry to back up a pitch instead of guessing at it.*

* [StatCite](https://statcite.com) **`C`** - Official economic statistics with full citations, plus a verifier that checks any claimed figure against the source. *Use case: Checking whether a GDP figure quoted in an article actually matches the official source, pulling a fully cited statistic for a report.*
* [Tastewise](https://tastewise.io) **`C`** - Answers food and beverage trend questions from menu, recipe, social, and consumer-research data across the US, UK, and other markets. *Use case: Checking how matcha performs on US menus versus social posts, building a concept brief for a Gen Z snack, comparing flavor demand between two regions.*

* [ThoughtLeaders - YouTube Data](https://thoughtleaders.ai) **`C`** - Searches indexed YouTube transcripts, daily channel and video performance history, and detected brand mentions tagged organic or sponsored, across millions of channels. *Use case: Finding channels that named a competitor last quarter, checking a creator's view trend before a sponsorship, quoting the transcript moment a product appeared.*
* [Tracxn](https://tracxn.com) **`C`** - Data intelligence, directly in your Claude. *Use case: Pulling a piece of data intelligence into a conversation instead of switching to another tool.*
* [Viberate music data](https://www.viberate.com) - Viberate provides music industry analytics on artists, tracks, festivals, and charts sourced from Spotify, TikTok, and airplay data. *Use case: Pull an artist's streaming and TikTok trend data to support a booking or marketing decision.*
* [Zint](https://www.zint.io) **`C`** - UK company data, financials, and news. *Use case: Pulling UK company financials, researching director and ownership data, monitoring corporate news signals.*

### Data Engineering and Pipelines

* [AgentContext](https://www.fivetran.ai) **`C`** - Exposes curated business context from connected enterprise systems, including data sources, schemas, concepts, knowledge bases, and analytical metadata, for agent queries. *Use case: Resolving which warehouse table backs a reported metric, giving a coding agent schema context for a query, searching internal knowledge before answering.*
* [Artie](https://www.artie.com) **`C`** - Creates and runs change-data-capture pipelines from PostgreSQL, MySQL, MongoDB, and other databases into cloud warehouses, handling table selection, backfills, and schema changes. *Use case: Replicating three production tables into Snowflake, backfilling six months of orders, pausing a staging pipeline during a database upgrade.*
* [atlan](https://atlan.com) **`C`** - Enterprise data catalog for searching assets, traversing end-to-end lineage, reading governed definitions and glossaries, executing SQL, curating metadata, and checking data quality. *Use case: Tracing a dashboard metric back to its source tables, checking a glossary definition before writing a query, flagging a table whose quality checks failed.*
* [Bruin](https://getbruin.com) **`C`** - Operates Bruin Cloud data pipelines: run history, execution logs, asset health, warehouse spend, triggered runs, backfills, and connection management. *Use case: Diagnosing a pipeline step that failed overnight, rerunning a single asset with its downstream dependencies, breaking down last month's warehouse spend by project.*
* [Coupler.io](https://www.coupler.io) - Pull data from hundreds of sources. *Use case: Cross-platform data aggregation, building dashboards from multiple SaaS tools.*
* [DataHub](https://datahub.com) - Connect AI agents to enterprise data and context. *Use case: Data discovery, cataloging, lineage tracking, governed access to enterprise datasets.*
* [Dataslayer mcp](https://www.dataslayer.ai) **`C`** - Connect data from all your data sources to analyze it with Claude. *Use case: Combining numbers from two different data sources into one answer, spotting a discrepancy between two systems.*
* [dbt](https://www.getdbt.com) - Explore, query, and build with dbt projects. *Use case: Analytics engineering, data transformation, modeling and testing data pipelines.*
* [Decube](https://www.decube.io) **`C`** - Data Context for Governance Team. *Use case: Checking the context behind a dataset before it goes into a governance review.*
* [Extract](https://www.extract.to) **`C`** - Manages ELT pipelines on Extract: sources, destinations, connections, and stream selection, with sample data, run history, failure diagnosis, and manual runs. *Use case: Wiring a new database source to a warehouse destination, sampling a stream before enabling it, diagnosing why last night's sync failed.*
* [Jedify](https://jedify.com) - Exposes a governed context graph of an organization's metrics, entities, business rules, and terminology, kept synchronized with the underlying source systems. *Use case: Resolving what a company means by active customer, checking which system owns a revenue metric, answering a question without knowing the underlying tables.*
* [Matia MCP](https://matia.io) **`C`** - Traces end-to-end data lineage and diagnoses issues across ingestion, reverse ETL, observability, and catalog in a Matia workspace. *Use case: Tracing which upstream source feeds a broken dashboard metric, checking whether last night's ingestion job completed, listing what breaks if a column is renamed.*
* [Mind One](https://wearemind.io) **`C`** - Creates and maintains schema-defined data sheets, organizes them into collections and workspaces, and keeps reusable value lists consistent across an organization. *Use case: Converting a loose supplier spreadsheet into a schema-defined sheet, grouping related sheets into one workspace collection, updating a shared list of product categories.*
* [Monte Carlo](https://www.montecarlodata.com) - Data and AI observability. *Use case: Detecting data freshness, schema, volume, and quality incidents across warehouses, lakes, and ML pipelines.*
* [Nexadata](https://www.nexadata.com) **`C`** - Connects to a Nexadata environment to discover pipelines, build and run data integration jobs, inspect run history, and pass runtime variables. *Use case: Kicking off a nightly integration job, checking which pipeline run failed overnight, passing a runtime variable before a rerun.*
* [Peaka](https://www.peaka.com) **`C`** - The fast lane to AI-ready data. *Use case: Getting data into an AI-ready state quickly instead of waiting on a manual pipeline.*
* [Prefect](https://www.prefect.io) - Inspects Prefect Cloud workflows, diagnoses failed, late or stuck runs, reviews workspace and worker health, and searches current Prefect documentation and release notes. *Use case: Finding why a nightly flow stalled, checking whether any workers went offline, reading the release notes before upgrading a deployment.*
* [Qmutator](https://qmutator.com) **`C`** - Your Qmutator workspace in chat — onboard product feeds, clean catalog data, and ship QFlow pipelines in plain language. *Use case: Fixing a bad product feed before it goes live, cleaning up duplicate entries in a catalog, describing a data pipeline in plain language instead of building it by hand.*
* [Senzing](https://senzing.com) **`C`** - Entity resolution: point Claude at your data, it does the work, and you have results today. *Use case: Matching two customer records that are actually the same person under different spellings, cleaning up a dataset full of duplicate entities before a merge.*
* [Sequentum MCP](https://sequentum.com) **`C`** - Turn the web into AI-ready enterprise data. *Use case: Pulling data from a set of web pages into an AI-ready format instead of copying it by hand.*
* [Streamkap](https://streamkap.com) **`C`** - Build, monitor, and troubleshoot real-time change-data-capture pipelines. *Use case: Debugging a lagging CDC pipeline, monitoring data freshness across source-to-warehouse syncs, standing up a pipeline for a database migration.*
* [Supermetrics](https://supermetrics.com) - Marketing performance data. *Use case: Aggregating marketing metrics across ad platforms, reporting automation.*
* [SyncWith](https://syncwith.com) **`C`** - Query your marketing & commerce data in Claude. *Use case: Checking how a marketing campaign's numbers compare to last month's commerce data without opening a spreadsheet.*
* [Syntitan](https://syntitan.ai) **`C`** - Score, refine, and reproduce AI-ready data. *Use case: Cleaning a dataset before model training, scoring data quality across sources, regenerating a reproducible dataset for retraining.*
* [Weld](https://weld.app) **`C`** - Creates and monitors Weld ETL pipelines that ingest data into a warehouse, transform it with SQL, and sync results back to business tools. *Use case: Setting up a nightly sync from a CRM into the warehouse, debugging a failed transform, pushing a cleaned customer list into an ad platform.*
* [Windsor.ai](https://windsor.ai) - Connect 325+ marketing data sources. *Use case: Marketing attribution, cross-channel performance analysis.*

### Databases and Warehouses

* [BEEM](https://www.beemdata.com) **`C`** - Browse, query, and analyze your BEEM data. *Use case: Browsing a dataset for a specific record, running a query to filter it down, analyzing the results for a report.*
* [Blaze SQL](https://www.blazesql.com) **`C`** - BlazeSQL connects Claude to SQL databases and data warehouses to run queries and build a business intelligence layer with visualizations, dashboards, and scheduled reports. *Use case: Query a production database in plain language and generate a scheduled revenue dashboard without hand-writing SQL.*
* [BlockchainQuery](https://www.blockchainquery.com) - Blockchain data across 60+ networks. *Use case: Querying on-chain data, analyzing transaction history, monitoring wallet activity across multiple chains.*
* [Blockscout](https://www.blockscout.com) - Analyze Blockchain data with a block explorer. *Use case: Smart contract verification, transaction tracing, token analytics.*
* [Cayra MCP](https://cayra.io) **`C`** - Answers plain-language questions against a connected business database, running multi-turn analytical queries across existing tables and views without SQL. *Use case: Asking which products sold most last month, following up to split that answer by region, listing overdue invoices by customer.*
* [ClickHouse](https://clickhouse.com) - Query and explore your ClickHouse Cloud data. *Use case: Analytical SQL over ClickHouse Cloud, schema exploration, ad-hoc OLAP queries from chat.*
* [Commercient Data Lake \ Data Hub](https://www.commercient.com/data-replication/data-lake) **`C`** - Connect to the Commercient Data Lake to query, export, and manage your ERP business data directly in Claude, with SQL reads, record CRUD, document search, aggregates, and time travel auditing. *Use case: Running a SQL query against ERP data instead of exporting a spreadsheet first, checking what a record looked like last quarter with time travel auditing, searching for a document buried in the data lake.*
* [contextflo](https://contextflo.com) **`C`** - Queries PostgreSQL, Snowflake, BigQuery, Redshift, MySQL, ClickHouse, and Databricks through a managed context layer with access controls and query visibility. *Use case: Asking for last quarter's active accounts in plain language, checking which definition a metric uses, reviewing which queries a teammate ran.*
* [Google Cloud BigQuery](https://cloud.google.com/bigquery) - Analytical insights from Google BigQuery. *Use case: Large-scale data analysis, SQL queries against data warehouses, business intelligence.*
* [Hgraph](https://hgraph.com) **`C`** - Query live Blockchain data on any supported network with GraphQL, SQL, and JSON-RPC through one connector. *Use case: Checking a wallet's balance on a supported network, pulling an address's recent transactions, running a custom query through GraphQL, SQL, or JSON-RPC.*
* [Intelas](https://intelas.com) **`C`** - Access your Intelas data from Claude. *Use case: Pulling up a specific record without opening a separate app.*
* [Loam Data](https://www.loamdata.com) **`C`** - Stores uploaded CSV, Excel, Parquet, TSV, and JSON business data and answers questions against the underlying rows under the connected account's permissions. *Use case: Asking which region led revenue last quarter, diffing this month's export against last month's, checking return rates by product.*
* [Loopthink](https://www.loopthink.ai) **`C`** - Governed access to your company's databases, CRMs and internal APIs — one connector, with per-group entitlements and field-level masking applied before any result reaches Claude. *Use case: Looking up a customer record in the CRM without seeing another team's masked fields, pulling a number from an internal database that only a manager's group can access.*
* [mcpMyAdmin](https://www.mcpmyadmin.com) **`C`** - Queries 40 databases and warehouses — PostgreSQL, MySQL, Oracle, MongoDB, Snowflake, BigQuery and more — in plain English, returning a chart and the SQL behind it. *Use case: Charting last quarter's signups from a production PostgreSQL replica, checking the generated SQL before trusting a figure, comparing order counts across two warehouses.*
* [Microsoft Dataverse](https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/power-platform/dataverse) - Query and manage Power Platform data. *Use case: Reading and writing Dataverse tables, building data-backed Power Platform apps, enterprise data operations.*
* [Snowflake](https://www.snowflake.com) - Structured and unstructured data. *Use case: Data warehousing, cross-cloud analytics, data sharing.*
* [Soracom Query](https://soracom.io) **`C`** - Runs SQL queries against Soracom data in Japanese or global coverage, inspects the available schema, checks query usage, and downloads full result exports. *Use case: Checking which tables exist before writing a query, running a SQL summary over last month's records, downloading the full export for offline review.*
* [SQD](https://sqd.dev) **`C`** - Query validated onchain data across 200+ blockchains. *Use case: Pulling historical transaction data for a token across chains, auditing smart contract activity, building a cross-chain analytics dashboard.*
* [Ultipa](https://www.ultipa.com) - Graph database and analytics. *Use case: Running GQL graph queries and algorithms, managing Ultipa Cloud instances, graph-based data exploration.*
* [Zite](https://www.zite.com) **`C`** - Manage and query Zite data. *Use case: Querying custom databases in natural language, creating and updating records, building data from uploaded documents.*

### Geospatial and Industry Data

* [Alloy Robotics](https://www.usealloy.ai) **`C`** - Analyze robot fleet data. *Use case: Comparing battery performance across a robot fleet, spotting a robot with an unusual error rate, checking uptime trends over the past week.*
* [Apartmentiq](https://www.apartmentiq.io) **`C`** - Integrates daily data on 40M units into powerful tools for market surveys, market research, and revenue management. *Use case: Checking comparable rents for a submarket, building a market survey from daily unit-level data, adjusting pricing as part of a revenue management review.*

* [BigGeo AI](https://biggeo.com) **`C`** - Answers questions about locations and how they relate using real-world spatial data instead of manual map or dataset searching. *Use case: Checking how far a site sits from competing locations, comparing two neighbourhoods on what surrounds them, finding where service areas overlap.*
* [Carpedia](https://www.carpedia.com.br) **`C`** - Looks up Brazilian vehicle specifications, standard and optional equipment, maintenance schedules, registration rankings, and FIPE reference prices with history. *Use case: Comparing trim levels on fuel consumption, checking which services a manual schedules at 40,000 km, estimating running costs including state IPVA.*

* [CivilQuants](https://civilquants.com) **`C`** - Software for producing civil engineering bills of quantities using the CESMM4, NRM2, SMM7, and MMHW standards. *Use case: Generate a bill of quantities for a civil works project from a set of drawings.*
* [Companion.energy](https://companion.energy) **`C`** - The platform for power pioneers. *Use case: Asking a first question to see what the platform can surface.*
* [Cotality](https://cotality.com) **`C`** - Looks up property ownership and characteristics by address, climate risk scores for portfolios, and home price trends and forecasts by market. *Use case: Pulling ownership details for an address before an appraisal, scoring a portfolio for flood exposure, checking price forecasts ahead of a lending decision.*
* [Datalastic](https://datalastic.com) **`C`** - Track vessels and access live and historical maritime data, including positions, ship details, ownership, ports, inspections, companies, engines, and sea routes and more. *Use case: Checking where a vessel is right now before a shipment arrives, pulling ownership history on a ship, reviewing a port's past inspection records.*

## Marketing and Sales

443 connectors.

### Advertising and Paid Media

* [Ad Superpowers](https://adsuperpowers.ai) **`C`** - Reads and, with confirmation, writes across Meta, Google Ads, Analytics 4, Search Console, Tag Manager, Merchant Center, LinkedIn, and TikTok accounts, logging every action. *Use case: Answering one cross-channel performance question instead of opening eight dashboards, creating a new ad left paused for review, checking the audit log of recent changes.*
